# Causal and receiver judgments — producer profiles, rows 0–39

[Population and scope](../causal-judgments.md) · [Relationship guide](../README.md) · [Scientific account](../../BWP-S.md)

Native row order is retained. A negative or unresolved record remains a substantive judgment; a directed record creates no unlisted reverse. Shared passages link to their complete exact values.

<a id="BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-001"></a>
## BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-001

<dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p><a href="producer_profiles-001.md#BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-001">BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-001</a></p></dd><dt>Producer</dt><dd><p><a href="../property-profiles/properties-001.md#EBPM-EBPM-001">EBPM:EBPM-001</a></p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PURPOSE</p></dd><dt>Native locator</dt><dd><dl><dt>Path</dt><dd><p>EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json</p></dd><dt>Bytes</dt><dd><p>1122330</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>99fa73496fc8d720c8b5f61f6ec85837b70287786ebde4422a1ea0bf0a41b60e</p></dd><dt>Json pointer</dt><dd><p>/properties/0</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Native meaning</dt><dd><dl><dt>Property name</dt><dd><p>End-to-end purposes and beneficiaries</p></dd><dt>Current status</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Identify the intended result, its recipients and the end-to-end work that produces it. Make competing outcome claims visible before choosing measures or redesigning tasks; a purchaser’s preference is not automatically every affected person’s interest.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>A proposed change can be judged against an explicit outcome and identified recipients, rather than only a department’s activity count.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Recipients, performers and sponsors compare what they regard as successful completion and record unresolved differences.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p>An identifiable beneficiary or affected constituency; a feasible way to resolve competing objectives; evidence of actual results.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>For a small, already understood service, a short agreed purpose and completion statement plus sample cases is sufficient; no portfolio exercise is required.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Responsible managers can commit resources; beneficiaries and affected workers contribute outcome evidence. Analysts cannot decide whose interests prevail merely by drawing the boundary.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Forward guard</dt><dd><p>The actual receiving task consumes the declared outcome, protected consequence, priority or unit as its objective or interpretation boundary.</p></dd><dt>Forward epm</dt><dd><ol><li><p>8</p></li><li><p>12</p></li><li><p>31</p></li><li><p>39</p></li><li><p>41</p></li><li><p>43</p></li><li><p>44</p></li><li><p>51</p></li><li><p>52</p></li><li><p>53</p></li><li><p>63</p></li><li><p>64</p></li><li><p>65</p></li><li><p>66</p></li><li><p>68</p></li><li><p>75</p></li><li><p>77</p></li><li><p>83</p></li><li><p>84</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Return epm</dt><dd><ol><li><p>8</p></li><li><p>39</p></li><li><p>43</p></li><li><p>51</p></li><li><p>52</p></li><li><p>53</p></li><li><p>63</p></li><li><p>64</p></li><li><p>65</p></li><li><p>75</p></li><li><p>77</p></li><li><p>83</p></li><li><p>84</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Forward ewm</dt><dd><ol><li><p>3</p></li><li><p>4</p></li><li><p>5</p></li><li><p>19</p></li><li><p>25</p></li><li><p>28</p></li><li><p>30</p></li><li><p>53</p></li><li><p>57</p></li><li><p>63</p></li><li><p>77</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Return ewm</dt><dd><ol><li><p>3</p></li><li><p>4</p></li><li><p>5</p></li><li><p>19</p></li><li><p>25</p></li><li><p>28</p></li><li><p>30</p></li><li><p>53</p></li><li><p>57</p></li><li><p>63</p></li><li><p>77</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Return guard</dt><dd><p>The named receiving task has actually produced and communicated a task-specific requirement, checked discrepancy, comparison result or evidence limit; the native source owner consumes it to select, explain, hold, narrow or revise its own stated function. Mere input consumption, a hypothetical requested change, or a silently reversed arrow does not meet this guard.</p></dd><dt>Return consequence</dt><dd><p>The delivered result changes the adequacy or stated scope of the source account/use. It does not retroactively change operational facts, create authority, authenticate a sender, establish an outside effect, or require a general platform.</p></dd><dt>Remaining premises</dt><dd><p>An objective does not supply a log, an estimator, a deployed effect or universal commensurability.</p></dd><dt>Negative basis</dt><dd><p>Current explicit native memberships include the r6 question/case operand. Apply its scoped judgment and separate remaining premises. Other negative uses retain their native reason and can reopen for an actual changed operand.</p></dd><dt>Reentry</dt><dd><p>Reopen this bounded decision if a concrete native output or returned diagnosis changes a named receiver premise under an actual shared scope. A return from the receiver is not required. An adequate existing record, direct conversation, narrower question or non-use can be sufficient; no new service follows.</p></dd><dt>Return absence control</dt><dd><p>No additional return contribution is established unless the receiving task actually produces a relevant result, requirement, discrepancy or evidence limit, communicates it back, and the original producer consumes it in its own function. A missing or unused return excludes this orientation only. Mere forward consumption and automatic reciprocity are insufficient; an already adequate source may need no revision.</p></dd><dt>R3 negative basis history</dt><dd><dl><dt>Subject</dt><dd><dl><dt>Path</dt><dd><p>SCIENTIFIC_SUBJECT.json</p></dd><dt>Bytes</dt><dd><p>17842</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>42e4f00b6bc0b8131ecc15030cb1e3fd8ab4fd9036116cedf8813e6ba5356af0</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Profile</dt><dd><p><a href="producer_profiles-001.md#BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-001">BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-001</a></p></dd><dt>Field</dt><dd><p>negative_basis</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>R4 judgment ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="r4_interface_judgments-001.md#BWP-R4-J001">BWP-R4-J001</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>R5 judgment ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="r5_interface_judgments-001.md#BWP-R5-J001">BWP-R5-J001</a></p></li><li><p><a href="r5_interface_judgments-002.md#BWP-R5-J066">BWP-R5-J066</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>R5 prior negative basis</dt><dd><dl><dt>File</dt><dd><p>CAUSAL_FRONTIER_OWNER.json</p></dd><dt>Profile</dt><dd><p><a href="producer_profiles-001.md#BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-001">BWP-FRONTIER-EBPM-001</a></p></dd><dt>Field</dt><dd><p>negative_basis</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>R5 guard scope</dt><dd><p>Each r5 judgment has its own exact target-specific guard, including constructed-model or prospective-definition bounds.
